Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [191] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-Lock，首个用于生成互锁装配体的强化学习框架，通过结构化动作分块与MCTS引导的策略-价值学习，高效探索大规模组合搜索空间，在现有方法失效或耗时过长的挑战性案例中仍能有效生成互锁装配体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁装配体仅通过几何排列连接部件，无需胶水或钉子等外部连接件，具有广泛实际应用和结构稳定性。现有方法依赖手工设计的搜索启发式，在复杂场景下耗时长甚至无法找到有效解。作者观察到互锁装配体生成本质上是一个序列决策问题（智能体反复决定每个体素分配给哪个部件），因此提出用强化学习来求解。

Method: RL-Lock框架将互锁装配体生成建模为强化学习问题，智能体逐步决策每个体素的部件分配。该方法结合结构化动作分块（structured action chunking）与MCTS（蒙特卡洛树搜索）引导的策略-价值学习，以高效地在巨大的组合搜索空间中导航，从而无需依赖手工设计的搜索启发式。

Result: 实验表明，RL-Lock能够有效生成互锁装配体，尤其在现有方法耗时过长甚至无法找到有效解的挑战性案例中表现出色。

Conclusion: 将互锁装配体生成问题重新定义为序列决策问题并采用强化学习方案是可行且有效的，RL-Lock为这一方向提供了首个不依赖手工启发式的学习型解决方案。

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [192] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JouleShare框架，通过离线重放测量请求级精确Shapley能源分配作为基准，并用轻量校准模型JCalib在线预测，大幅缩小token比例分配与公平分配之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量LLM推理服务虽然提升了吞吐量，但GPU功率遥测是聚合的，无法为可持续性报告、成本分摊和工作负载分析提供请求级能源分摊。现有推理能源基准只报告模型级/阶段级/token级能耗，缺乏实测的请求级地面真值，导致实际使用的分配规则与公平分配之间的偏差未知。

Method: JouleShare由两部分组成：1) 离线测量装置，在vLLM下重放请求子集，整合GPU功率遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能源值作为地面真值；2) 轻量校准模型JCalib，从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley份额，供在线服务时使用。

Result: 在16个模型/工作负载组合中，token比例分配与精确Shapley的平均归一化L1偏差在静态批处理下为0.440，连续批处理下为0.458，且跨三种数据中心GPU重现。JCalib将此误差降至静态批处理0.116、连续批处理0.177，优于不可在线的独立测量基线，同时保持精确的批量能效。采样Shapley可扩展到更大请求组，gap持续存在，单一离线校准仍是最准确的可用规则。

Conclusion: Token分配不能可靠地作为批量执行下边际能源的代理指标，基于实测Shapley地面真值可以对低成本请求特征进行校准，实现更公平的能源分配。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [193] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba是一种结构化状态空间模型，通过引入基于三节点网络基序启发的低秩循环通路来增强Mamba，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时改善状态维度间的显式交互，在长序列外推、语言建模和脑机接口解码任务中均优于标准Mamba。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力在长序列建模中呈二次方复杂度，Mamba虽提供线性时间方案，但其对角状态转移限制了状态维度间的显式交互，压缩了模型捕捉跨维度动态关系的能力。

Method: 提出Motif-Mamba，在Mamba基础上增加一个基序约束的低秩循环通路：将隐藏状态投影到紧凑的动态子空间，施加三节点网络基序引导的交互，再将动态映射回原始状态空间。该设计增强跨维度通信，同时保留Mamba的线性时间递归结构。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准测试和脑机接口解码实验中，Motif-Mamba相比Mamba骨干网络均取得一致性改进，证明基序引导的低秩动态作为长程序列建模的结构先验是有效的。

Conclusion: 基序引导的低秩循环通路为长程序列建模提供了有效的结构先验，在保持线性时间效率的同时增强了状态维度的交互表达能力。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [194] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova是一个端到端的JIT编译器，通过捕获eager执行、统一前后向传播为值语义方言，并结合Analytic Configurator（零搜索时间推导最优调度）和结构哈希运行时合成细粒度内核，实现对底层硬件映射的绝对控制。在RTX 3060上，Nova在TF32矩阵乘法上匹敌或略超cuBLAS和XLA，模型吞吐量比PyTorch高10.6%、比XLA高4.4%，并将内存占用降低29%，使其能在12GB消费级GPU上训练PyTorch因OOM失败而无法训练的1.44亿参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型规模化时的性能高度依赖于高级数学操作到物理硬件的映射效率。现有高层张量框架虽然提供灵活的模型设计抽象，但其eager执行模型天然缺乏全局图可见性以及对硬件和内存的细粒度控制，无法原生最大化物理硬件利用率。因此需要一种能够跨操作边界融合算子、优化复杂内存层级、并精细到寄存器级别的自动化编译方案来弥补这一鸿沟。

Method: Nova采用三条核心设计：(1) 捕获eager执行并将前向与反向传播统一为单一的值语义（value-semantic）方言，从而解锁激进的whole-graph优化；(2) 利用Analytic Configurator基于算术强度（arithmetic intensity）确定性推导最优执行调度，将搜索时间降至零；(3) 依托结构哈希（structural hashing）运行时，直接从计算结构合成细粒度内核。整体构成一个自动化的端到端JIT编译器，实现跨操作融合、内存层级优化以及寄存器级调优。

Result: 在RTX 3060上的评测表明：对TF32矩阵乘法，Nova在大多数形状下匹敌或略超cuBLAS和XLA，同时保持严格的<5e-4相对误差。在4200万参数模型层面，Nova的吞吐量比PyTorch高10.6%、比XLA高4.4%，且不损失数值保真度。更重要的是，Nova相对PyTorch将内存占用减少高达29%，从而在12GB消费级GPU上以17,900 tokens/s成功训练1.44亿参数模型，而PyTorch在同一硬件上遭遇OOM失败。

Conclusion: Nova证明了通过端到端JIT编译配合确定性调度推导与结构驱动内核合成，可以在无需搜索耗时的情况下同时实现更高的吞吐、更低的内存占用和更强的可扩展性，使消费级GPU能够训练此前无法容纳的大规模模型，展示了在高性能深度学习编译领域兼顾效率与精度的可行路径。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [195] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN是一个统一的工具包和交互式Web UI，用于检测检索增强、智能体和基于记忆的大语言模型系统中的上下文幻觉（忠实但不受证据支持的流畅响应）。它统一了三种检测器范式（表征探测、不确定性估计和评判式验证）以及生成前查询可回答性任务，支持白盒和黑盒设置下的响应级和跨度级检查，并提供了轻量级插件设计用于添加新检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强、智能体和记忆接地的大语言模型系统容易出现上下文幻觉，即生成的响应虽然流畅合理但与提供的证据不符。现有检测方法分散在不同范式（表征探测、不确定性估计、评判式验证）中，缺乏统一的接口、配置系统和评估流程，且缺乏对生成前查询可回答性的预判能力。

Method: SIRIN整合了三种检测器范式（表征探测、不确定性估计和评判式验证）和查询可回答性任务到一个统一界面、配置系统和评估流水线中。支持白盒和黑盒设置下的响应级与跨度级检查，Web UI允许对用户提供的上下文-查询-答案三元组进行实时分析，包括幻觉评分、不支持跨度高亮和检测器并排比较，采用轻量级插件设计以扩展新检测器。

Result: 在幻觉检测、查询可回答性以及作为长期记忆系统中的忠实性门控三个任务上进行了演示验证，源代码已公开。

Conclusion: SIRIN提供一个统一、可扩展且交互式的基础设施，用于检测和检查上下文幻觉，涵盖多种检测范式和LLM系统类型，并支持作为记忆系统的忠实性门控，为社区提供了可复用的开源工具。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [196] [Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00035)
*Brian Song,Michael A. Lepori,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言模型（VLM）中语言诱导的视觉表征重编码现象，发现两种具体实例：抽象参考表征（标识目标相关对象）和属性调制（放大目标相关属性），并通过因果干预证明两者均影响模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究大多将VLM中的视觉表征视为静态的视觉信息仓库，由语言表征操纵使用。然而VLM实际上面临许多目标导向的视觉任务（如分类、搜索），其视觉表征如何被语言动态调制仍不清楚，这推动了对跨模态处理更动态视角的探索。

Method: 通过提取对比引导向量（contrastive steering vectors）识别抽象参考表征，并验证其在不同对象、任务情境乃至从合成到自然图像上的泛化性。同时考察语言提示引起的属性调制现象，最后通过因果干预实验证明属性调制介导了VLM的响应分布。

Result: 发现两种语言诱导的视觉表征重编码实例：(1) 抽象参考表征与目标相关性判断因果相关，且能跨对象、跨任务、跨图像域泛化；(2) 后续层选择性地放大目标相关属性的视觉表征。因果干预证明了这两种机制介导模型输出。

Conclusion: VLM的视觉令牌并非静态的信息仓库，而是被语言动态调制以支持语言表达的任务需求，这支持了跨模态处理更动态的观点，提示视觉表征在VLM中是目标导向、灵活可重编码的。

Abstract: Goal-directed visual processing is a hallmark of human visual intelligence, resulting in representations that support downstream tasks such as categorization or search. Though vision-language models (VLMs) are often faced with these same tasks, their ability to recode visual representations when presented with goal-directed language remains poorly characterized. Indeed, prior work largely treats visual representations in VLMs as static repositories of visual information that are manipulated by language representations. In the present work, we provide evidence for two concrete instances of language-induced recoding of visual representations. First, we identify an abstract reference representation that denotes which objects are goal-relevant under a natural language prompt. We extract contrastive steering vectors corresponding to this reference representation and demonstrate that they are causally implicated in model predictions. These reference representations are abstract in that they generalize to different objects, different task contexts, and even from synthetic to naturalistic images. Second, we demonstrate language-induced attribute modulation: later layers selectively amplify goal-relevant attributes in visual representations of objects. We demonstrate this phenomenon across a range of different prompts. Finally, we provide a causal intervention that demonstrates that attribute modulation mediates a VLM's response distribution. Together, our results support a more dynamic account of cross-modality processing in VLMs -- rather than vision tokens serving as static repositories of information, they are modulated to support queries articulated in language.

</details>


### [197] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RagTester是一个针对RAG（检索增强生成）系统的自动化端到端测试方法，通过生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出，使用LLM作为评判器评估答案，在72,000次测试执行中检测到21,633个失败，比基线方法多6.6%，并在24个配置中的20个上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统使LLM能够利用外部和领域特定知识，但其可靠性依赖于生成模型、嵌入模型、检索机制和提示构造策略之间的复杂交互。目前缺乏自动化的端到端测试方法来评估这些组件交互可能导致的失败。

Method: RagTester采用自动化端到端测试流程：生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出；执行测试；使用LLM作为评判器评估答案。其测试生成策略针对复杂段落、不支持的查询和文档覆盖标准三个维度。

Result: 使用8个LLM和6个嵌入模型组成24个兼容配置进行评估，共执行72,000次测试，检测到21,633个失败（比基线多6.6%），在24个配置中20个优于基线。检测到的失败包括不准确的检索、不支持的答案、检索上下文使用不完整以及解释复杂段落的困难。

Conclusion: 面向覆盖的测试生成能有效暴露检索与生成组件交互导致的失败，并支持在部署前评估RAG配置。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [198] [H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00065)
*Shusen Zhang,Junyi Hu,Ye Feng,Ziteng Wang,Zhaoyuan Pan,Guosheng Dong,Xiaojun Yuan,Jiangshou Hong,Xiangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出H+ Embedding，一种统一的多粒度检索器，在全局向量与token级交互之间引入上下文相关的短语作为检索单元，通过预测可变长度短语分区、保留未覆盖token为单例，并结合重要性引导的单元选择与加权MaxSim交互，在16个科学、医学和双语任务中实现检索质量与成本的折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业术语密集的检索场景（尤其是医学领域）依赖保留多词实体、缩写、数值约束和组合概念，但现有表示处于两个极端：单向量检索器过度压缩局部相关性信号，而token级late interaction保留每个tokenizer子词导致巨大的索引、存储和评分成本。因此作者提出疑问：上下文相关的短语能否作为介于全局向量和token之间的检索单元？

Method: 提出H+ Embedding统一多粒度检索器：1）预测可变长度的短语分区作为检索单元；2）未覆盖的token保留为单例单元；3）应用重要性引导的单元选择策略；4）采用加权MaxSim交互进行最终评分。整个框架将短语检索与全局检索集成在统一架构中。

Result: 在16个科学、医学和双语任务上，短语检索分支相比全局检索分支在macro nDCG@10上提升6.91；同时几乎达到Token级方法的性能，但文档向量数量减少13.7%；在中度向量预算下优于基于内容无关的分组规则。

Conclusion: 上下文相关的短语交互为实际检索系统提供了介于全局压缩与token级交互之间的中间质量-成本权衡点，验证了短语作为一种有效检索单元的可行性。

Abstract: Terminology-intensive retrieval, especially in medical settings, depends on preserving multi-word entities, abbreviations, numerical constraints, and compositional concepts. However, existing representations lie at two extremes: single-vector retrievers often over-compress local relevance signals, while token-level late interaction retains every tokenizer subword at substantial indexing, storage, and scoring cost. This mismatch raises a natural question: can context-dependent phrases provide a useful retrieval unit between global vectors and tokens? We introduce H+ Embedding, a unified multi-granularity retriever that predicts variable-length phrase partitions, preserves uncovered tokens as singletons, and applies importance-guided unit selection with weighted MaxSim interaction. Across 16 scientific, medical, and bilingual tasks, its phrase retrieval branch exceeds the global retrieval branch by 6.91 macro nDCG@10. It also nearly matches Token while using 13.7% fewer document vectors and outperforms content-independent grouping rules under moderate vector budgets. Context-dependent phrase interaction therefore provides an intermediate quality-cost point between global compression and token-level interaction for practical retrieval systems.

</details>


### [199] [Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00101)
*Banruo Liu,Haoran Qiu,Íñigo Goiri,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于GitHub Copilot在2026年6月的生产级采样数据（320万用户、1300万会话、7.61亿次LLM调用、95万亿token），首次对AI编码智能体的工作负载进行了规模化特征刻画，揭示了与聊天机器人截然不同的工作负载特性，并提出了一种轻量级空闲时间预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体（如GitHub Copilot、Claude Code、Codex）将多步LLM推理与工具执行交错进行，形成了与聊天机器人不同的独特工作负载。然而，现有LLM服务系统主要基于聊天场景设计，缺乏对智能体工作负载的实证理解，导致系统资源编排和架构设计可能不适配。

Method: 对GitHub Copilot在2026年6月的生产轨迹数据进行大规模采样分析（32M用户、13M会话、761M次LLM调用、95T tokens），从会话结构、KV缓存命中率、token消耗分布、时间跨度、工具调用模式等多个维度刻画工作负载特征，并设计了一个轻量级空闲时间预测器。

Result: 发现智能体编码会话由稀疏的用户发起轮次组成，每轮展开为自主的智能体循环；轮内KV缓存命中率平均90%，但跨轮边界降至55%，且在模型切换或上下文压缩等事件后显著失效；工作负载在token消耗、时间跨度和工具调用上呈现长尾分布；智能体周转时间快速但用户空闲期长达数分钟，所设计的空闲时间预测器可捕获86-90%的总空闲时间。

Conclusion: 这些发现挑战了当前LLM服务系统的设计假设，并为智能体原生的基础设施提供了实证基础，指导未来系统在缓存管理、资源编排和空闲感知调度等方面的优化。

Abstract: AI coding agents like GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, and Codex interleave multi-step LLM inference with tool execution, creating a workload different from chatbots. We present the first production-scale characterization of this workload using sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026, comprising 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens.
  Our analysis reveals distinctive workload properties with important systems implications. For example, agentic coding sessions consist of sparse user-initiated turns, each unfolding into an autonomous agent loop of LLM calls almost always coupled with tool execution. This structure yields KV cache hit rates averaging 90% within a turn, but falling to 55\% across turn boundaries and drastically invalidated after events like model switches or context compaction. Diverse workflows and user behaviors are observed with variable and long-tailed token consumption, time span, and tool calls. We highlight the difference between quick agentic turnaround times and the minutes-long user idle periods at turn boundaries, and design a lightweight idle-time predictor that captures 86-90\% of total idle time, enabling proactive decisions for efficient resource orchestration. These findings challenge assumptions underlying current LLM-serving systems and provide an empirical foundation for agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [200] [Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00102)
*Shimaa Ahmed,Yiwei Cai,Mohsen Minaei,Rahul Rachuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bazaar，一个面向多属性拍卖的动态密封竞价基准，用于系统测试LLM在真实市场条件下的定价能力。测试了11个前沿LLM后发现：客户获取领先者与利润领先者不一致，需求冲击后排名再次翻转，且最强智能体也仅捕获不到最优利润的三分之一，表明LLM在agentic commerce中仍有巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic commerce正从概念走向落地基础设施，但LLM背后的智能体能否在真实市场中熟练定价（客户偏好隐藏、竞争对手实时适应、需求可能无预警变化）尚未被系统测试。

Method: 引入Bazaar基准，这是一个动态密封竞价下的多属性拍卖基准。尽管环境具有动态性，但基于闭式客户效用函数构建，支持精确评估。对来自四个提供商的11个前沿LLM进行系统测试。

Result: 在获客方面领先的智能体（如Gemini 3.1 Pro）往往不是利润领先者（如Opus 4.6）。需求冲击下排名再变：冲击前学习最快的智能体通常冲击后信念修正最慢，而Gemini 3.1 Pro恢复最快但利润并非领先。即使最强智能体也仅捕获不到事后最优利润的三分之一。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在agentic commerce中虽有进展，但定价能力离最优仍有显著差距，存在大量提升空间。

Abstract: Agentic commerce is moving from concept to deployed infrastructure: payment networks, retailers, and AI platforms are setting the stage for agents to transact on behalf of merchants and consumers. Yet whether the LLMs behind these agents can price competently in real markets, where customer preferences are hidden, competitors adapt in real time, and demand can shift without warning, has not been systematically tested. We introduce Bazaar, a dynamic sealed-bid benchmark for multi-attribute auction under these conditions. Despite its dynamics, the benchmark is grounded in closed-form customer utilities, enabling exact evaluation. Across 11 frontier LLMs from four providers, the leading agents on customer acquisition (e.g. Gemini 3.1 Pro) are often not the leading agents on profit (e.g. Opus 4.6). The ranking shifts again under demand shocks: agents that learned fastest pre-shock are typically the slowest to revise their beliefs afterwards, while Gemini 3.1 Pro recovers fastest despite not leading on profit. However, even the strongest agent captures less than a third of hindsight-optimal profit, suggesting current LLMs are progressing in agentic commerce but leave substantial headroom.

</details>


### [201] [Shared Organizational Memory for Enterprise Coding Agents: System Design and Deployment Snapshot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00122)
*Harsh Rao Dhanyamraju,Leonidas Raghav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个企业级共享组织记忆系统的生产部署情况，该系统将经验捕获作为编码工作流的平台级组成部分，自动收集任务相关的开发经验、经贡献者批准后整理为可复用的问答记忆，并进行安全隐私把关和检索服务，解决企业编码代理因知识缺失而不断重新发现开发经验的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业编码代理依赖工具和检索，但企业知识（内部DSL、专有平台、本地约定、近期修复、隐性工作流）经常不在公开训练数据和正式文档中。现有知识接口仅暴露已存储资源，仍依赖代理自行识别和显式记录经验，导致经验捕获与编码工作流脱节，开发经验被反复重新发现。

Method: 构建一个平台级的组织记忆系统：在执行编码任务时自动收集任务相关的经验数据，经贡献者审批确认后，将其整理为可复用的问答形式记忆；同时设置安全与隐私风险门控机制过滤敏感内容，并将记忆检索提供给未来的编码代理使用。

Result: 系统已投入生产环境部署，本文描述了完整的部署生命周期和运营快照；但对检索和编码任务的实际效果评估仍在进行中。

Conclusion: 该系统的核心贡献在于将经验捕获从代理的显式行为转变为平台级的基础设施能力，使知识沉淀自然融入编码工作流，但目前仍处于早期部署阶段，其对检索效果和编码任务的影响有待进一步评估。

Abstract: Enterprise coding agents rely on tools and retrieval, yet enterprise knowledge often remains outside public training data and formal documentation: internal DSLs, proprietary platforms, local conventions, recent fixes, and tacit workflows. Existing knowledge interfaces expose stored resources but still depend on agents recognizing and explicitly recording lessons worth reusing, disconnecting capture from the coding workflow and leaving development experience repeatedly rediscovered. We report an ongoing production deployment of a shared organizational memory system that makes capture a platform-level part of coding work: it collects task-adjacent experience with contributor approval, curates it into reusable question-answer memories, gates obvious security and privacy risks, and retrieves memories for future agents. This short paper describes the deployed lifecycle and an operational snapshot. Effects on retrieval and coding tasks remain under evaluation.

</details>


### [202] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentStream框架，用于评估自进化LLM智能体在现实流式任务场景中的表现，通过三种流式场景（隔离、顺序、交错）对五种自进化方法和三种基础模型进行组合评估，发现自进化的可靠性随流式场景变化、其收益受模型能力门控且与模型强度呈非单调关系，没有单一方法在所有场景和模型上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体研究主要采用独立评估方式，无法反映现实环境中智能体需要不断适应多样化且复杂任务流的流式场景，因此其行为模式尚不明确。作者希望填补这一研究空白，建立更符合实际的评估框架。

Method: AgentStream是一个统一评估框架：将智能体基准重组为可配置的任务流，在测试时实例化Isolated、Sequential和Interleaved三种流式场景，逐步改变任务流的规模范围和领域构成。在此基础上，对五种代表性自进化方法在三种前沿基础模型上进行组合评估，解耦模型能力、方法架构和流式场景对自进化效果的联合影响。

Result: 研究发现：自进化的可靠性在不同流式场景间存在差异；自进化的收益受模型能力门控，且与模型强度呈非单调关系；没有任何单一方法在所有模型和场景上表现占优。

Conclusion: 自进化智能体应在现实任务流而非孤立的单任务设置下进行评估。研究结果为跨模型和跨流式场景选择自进化方法提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream. Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [203] [TRACE-TS: Attribution-Grounded and Traceable Sensor-Language Reasoning for Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00200)
*Sparsh Rastogi,Tanmay Kumar,Baiyu Chen,Jatin Bedi,Zechen Li,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-TS框架，利用专家分类器的归因信息识别可穿戴传感器数据中的显著时空区域，构建带有明确证据来源的DAG推理轨迹，并训练紧凑语言模型通过门控交叉注意力生成这些轨迹，实现活动预测与可验证推理的联合输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语言模型的可穿戴传感器数据解释方法虽然能生成流畅的自然语言说明，但其推理过程与底层信号的联系薄弱，导致解释流畅却无法验证。这促使研究者开发一种结构化、信号可溯源的推理框架。

Method: TRACE-TS通过三个步骤实现信号可溯源的推理：(1) 利用专家分类器的归因结果识别显著的时空传感器区域；(2) 基于这些区域构建带有显式证据来源的有向无环图(DAG)推理轨迹；(3) 训练紧凑语言模型，通过门控交叉注意力机制融合传感器记忆token来生成推理轨迹。推理阶段无需归因计算或教师指导，模型联合输出活动预测与推理轨迹。同时提出SNM(Semantic Node Match)评估指标，利用LLM作为裁判在观察、推理、综合三个层次诊断推理保真度。

Result: 在七个可穿戴基准数据集上，TRACE-TS取得了所有评估方法中最优的平均准确率(84.43%)和F1值(81.24%)，相比最佳基于LLM的基线方法在F1上提升了17.96%。

Conclusion: TRACE-TS通过将信号归因与推理轨迹结合，有效解决了可穿戴传感器数据分析中解释缺乏可验证性的问题，验证了结构化、信号可溯源的推理生成在可穿戴行为理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Wearable sensors capture fine-grained motion patterns that support rich behavioral understanding, yet most existing methods reduce these signals to activity labels. Recent LM-based approaches generate natural-language explanations for sensor data, but their reasoning is weakly grounded in the underlying signal, leading to fluent yet unverifiable explanations. We introduce TRACE-TS (Traceable Reasoning with Attribution-Grounded Evidence), a framework for structured and signal-grounded reasoning over wearable time series. TRACE-TS uses attribution from an expert classifier to identify salient spatio-temporal sensor regions, uses them to construct DAG reasoning traces with explicit evidence provenance, and trains a compact language model to generate these traces through gated cross-attention over sensor memory tokens. At inference, the adapted model jointly outputs the activity prediction and its reasoning trace, without requiring attribution computation or teacher guidance. We introduce Semantic Node Match(SNM), an LLM-as-judge metric that diagnoses reasoning fidelity at the observation, inference, and synthesis levels, localizing hallucinated observations and broken evidence chains missed by standard NLG metrics. Across seven wearable benchmarks, TRACE-TS achieves the best average accuracy and F1 among all evaluated methods (84.43%/81.24%), and outperforms the best LLM-based baseline by 17.96% in F1. Our code is available at https://github.com/SparshRastogi/TRACE-TS.

</details>


### [204] [Personalizing Large Language Model Agents with Small Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00215)
*Dian Jin,Zhi Zhang,Huichao Li,Yihe Pan,Rundong Huang,Doudou Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FABLE是一种轻量级策略层，通过贝叶斯上下文汤普森采样在冻结的（可能是黑盒的）LLM智能体外部学习每个用户的执行策略，从而以在线方式实现个性化，无需微调底层模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体虽有检索记忆、调用工具、提问、调整风格等能力，但将这些执行决策适配到个体用户仍很困难。针对每个用户微调单独的LLM成本高昂，且对专有系统不可行；提示和记忆只能将用户信息暴露给智能体，而非根据反馈自适应地调整其执行决策。

Method: 将冻结智能体的个性化形式化为从仅对已执行动作可观测的标量反馈中在线学习每个用户的执行策略。FABLE将记忆、信息获取和响应决策分解为独立因子，使反馈只更新相关选择；在探索前通过外部指定的可行集过滤动作；使用贝叶斯上下文汤普森采样学习相对于固定默认-成本分数的用户特定残差偏好。同时提供校准变体、期望遗憾界、偏好不可识别性刻画和任意时间有效的虚假推广控制。

Result: 在线性残差奖励模型下，校准变体对最佳可行动作享有期望遗憾界。在个性化推理、受控反馈和可执行工具使用评估中，FABLE相比纯规则控制改善了多个偏好敏感行为，同时保持端到端任务性能的竞争力。

Conclusion: FABLE在无需微调底层LLM的前提下，以轻量策略层+在线学习框架实现了对冻结智能体的有效个性化，兼顾偏好适应、理论保证和任务性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can retrieve memory, call tools, ask clarifying questions, and vary response style, yet adapting these execution decisions to an individual user remains difficult. Fine-tuning a separate LLM is costly or impossible for proprietary systems, while prompts and memory primarily expose user information to the agent rather than adapt its execution decisions from feedback. We formulate personalization of a frozen agent as online learning of a per-user execution policy from scalar feedback observed only for the executed action. We propose FABLE (Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution), a lightweight policy layer outside a potentially black-box host agent. FABLE factorizes memory, information-acquisition, and response decisions so feedback updates related choices; filters actions through an externally specified feasible set before exploration; and learns user-specific residual preferences relative to a fixed default-and-cost score via Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling. Under a linear residual-reward model, a calibrated variant inherits an expected-regret bound against the best feasible action. We also characterize preferences unidentifiable under persistent feasibility constraints and provide anytime-valid false-promotion control. Across personalized-reasoning, controlled-feedback, and executable tool-use evaluations, FABLE improves several preference-sensitive behaviors relative to rule-only control while remaining competitive on end-to-end task performance.

</details>


### [205] [More Debate, Same Evidence: Structural Limits of Homogeneous Multi-Agent Groundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00243)
*Yuelyu Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多代理LLM评审团在groundedness验证任务中的表现，发现相对于单代理基准，多代理评审团的系统级准确率差异从+8.5%到-4.4%不等：两个数据集有可靠提升，一个数据集有可靠下降，三个数据集统计上不显著。且由于使用不同模型变体，这些差异反映的是整个系统而非单纯的辩论效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多代理LLM评审团被越来越广泛地采用，其核心假设是代理之间交换批评意见能提升判断质量。作者旨在检验这一假设在groundedness验证（判断声明是否被证据支持）这一具体任务上的有效性，因为该假设的成立与否直接影响多代理系统的设计决策。

Method: 在六个公开的事实核查和幻觉检测基准上，评估一个同质三代理评审团。将多代理评审团的系统级准确率与使用不同模型变体的固定单代理基准进行比较，从系统整体角度衡量性能差异，而非分离出因果关系中的辩论效应。

Result: 多代理评审团相对单代理基准在系统级准确率上的差异范围为+8.5%到-4.4%个百分点。六个基准中两个显示可靠增益，一个显示可靠损失，三个统计上无显著结论。结果表明多代理评审并非总是优于单代理。

Conclusion: 多代理评审团在groundedness验证中的表现因数据集而异，不支持"多代理总是更好"的假设。由于参考系统和评审团使用不同模型变体，所观察到的差异不能直接归因于辩论效应，需要更精细的实验设计来隔离因果影响。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are increasingly organized as multi-agent panels under the assumption that exchanging critiques improves judgment quality. We test this assumption for \emph{groundedness verification}, where a judge must determine whether a claim is supported by the supplied evidence. We evaluate a homogeneous three-agent panel on six public fact-verification and hallucination-detection benchmarks. Relative to a fixed single-agent reference, the panel's system-level accuracy difference ranges from $+8.5$ to $-4.4$ percentage points: two datasets show reliable gains, one shows a reliable loss, and three are statistically inconclusive. Because the reference and panel use different model variants, these differences characterize the complete systems rather than isolate a causal debate effect.

</details>


### [206] [Geometric Self-Supervised Pre-training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00270)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向路由问题（TSP）的几何自监督预训练框架。通过对2D空间坐标应用等距变换（旋转和轴向反射），模型在策略优化前学习空间不变性和全局相对距离分布，从而显著提升零样本外推能力。在TSP1,000大规模场景下，相较于从零训练的基线模型，生成了7.23%的行程长度改进，且比精确求解器Concorde快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）技术在求解路由问题（如TSP）上展现高效性，但基于强化学习的模型在扩展到高维实例时泛化能力严重受限。虽然自监督预训练已在计算机视觉和自然语言处理等领域成功缓解了类似问题，但路由图缺乏除2D空间坐标外的复杂拓扑属性，如何将其有效应用于此类场景仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 提出一个几何自监督预训练框架，核心思路是利用等距变换（旋转和轴向反射）构造自监督学习任务，使模型在策略优化阶段之前学习到空间不变性（spatial invariance）和全局相对距离分布（global relative distance distributions），从而获得鲁棒的结构化表示能力。

Result: 在TSP1,000的大规模零样本外推场景下，该策略相比从零训练的基线模型实现了7.23%的行程长度改进；同时在计算效率方面表现卓越，在大规模场景下相较精确求解器Concorde取得了高达两个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 几何自监督预训练是一种有效的NCO泛化增强手段，可在无需复杂拓扑特征的前提下，通过赋予模型空间不变性知识来显著提升大规模TSP等路由问题的求解质量与效率。代码和预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) techniques have emerged as a highly efficient alternative to traditional exact algorithms for solving routing problems such as the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). However, the generalization capabilities of these Reinforcement Learning-based models are severely hindered when scaling to high-dimensional instances. This issue has been mitigated in other domains, like computer vision and natural language processing, by adopting a self-supervised pre-training strategy. Nevertheless, its application to routing graphs, which lack complex topological attributes beyond 2D spatial coordinates, remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose a geometric self-supervised pre-training framework specifically designed to capture spatial invariance and global relative distance distributions. By applying isometric transformations, such as rotations and axial reflections, the model learns robust structural representations prior to the policy optimization phase. Empirical results demonstrate that this strategy consistently outperforms models trained from scratch (baselines), achieving a 7.23\% improvement in tour length for massive zero-shot extrapolation scenarios (TSP1,000). Furthermore, the proposed model exhibits remarkable computational efficiency, delivering speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over the exact solver Concorde at massive scales.
  The source code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/davidaguadocosano/TSP-GeoPretrain.git.

</details>


### [207] [RF-HOI: Recognize Human-Object Interaction with Radio Frequency Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00289)
*Lihao Wang,Linlu Gao,Jiacan Yu,Yanyu Lin,Yifan Yin,Jianxin Wang,Tianmin Shu,Renjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RF-HOI是首个仅使用射频（RF）信号进行人-物交互（HOI）识别的框架，通过融合毫米波雷达和RFID实现动作识别与目标识别的统一，并利用多模态RF数据合成模拟器解决训练数据不足问题，在实验中超越所有基线，逼近视觉模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的HOI识别方法面临隐私泄露和光照条件差两大核心挑战，需要一种不依赖摄像头、能在弱光环境下工作的替代方案。此外，仅使用单一射频模态不足以同时识别交互动作和交互对象，需要多模态融合。同时，多样化场景下的训练数据匮乏严重制约模型的泛化能力。

Method: RF-HOI提出了一种新颖的模态融合方法，将毫米波雷达与RFID相结合，实现动作识别和目标识别的并行处理。针对数据不足问题，开发了一个模拟器能够大规模合成多样化的多模态RF数据，在只需少量真实数据微调的情况下，即可获得良好的泛化性能。

Result: 实验结果表明RF-HOI在各项指标上均优于所有基线方法，性能逼近基于视觉的模型；多样化的合成训练数据能够显著提升系统在真实场景中的表现。

Conclusion: 多模态射频感知在隐私保护和稳健的HOI识别方面具有巨大的潜力，所提出的RF数据合成方法也有效解决了训练数据不足的瓶颈，为未来RF感知驱动的智能交互系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recognizing Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is essential for intelligent systems, underpinning applications in virtual and augmented reality, embodied AI, and assistive robotics. However, vision-based HOI methods face challenges in privacy concerns and poor light conditions. In this work, we introduce RF-HOI, the first framework that only uses radio frequency (RF) signals for HOI recognition. A key challenge of RF-HOI is that single-modality RF sensing is insufficient to recognize both actions and the objects being interacted with. RF-HOI addresses this through a novel modality fusion that combines mmWave radar and RFID, enabling simultaneous action recognition and target identification. Another challenge is limited training data across diverse setups, which impairs the generalizability of the recognition model. To overcome this, we develop a simulator that synthesizes multimodal RF data for diverse HOIs at scale, allowing us to fine-tune with only a small amount of real-world data. Experiment results show that RF-HOI outperforms all baselines, approaching vision model performance, and that our diverse synthetic training data can significantly boost our system's performance on real-world scenarios. These results highlight the potential of multimodal RF sensing for robust and privacy-preserving HOI recognition as well as the effectiveness of our RF data synthesis.

</details>


### [208] [WM-Cov: Test Adequacy for Interactive World-Model-Style Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00298)
*Jianxun Cui,Ping Wu,Stanisa Peric,Marko Milojkovic,Vladan Devedzic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型式自动驾驶交互测试充分性框架及WM-Cov评估层，将原始生成器输出转化为有效证据，通过覆盖率增长、有效失败发现等指标替代原有的原始失败或提示覆盖率，在TeraSim/SUMO和DriveArena实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶测试正从固定回放轨迹转向交互式世界模型/生成模拟器，场景演化取决于被测规划器，因此仅判断"能否生成危险 rollout"不够，需明确何种有效的闭环证据足以支持测试意图和停止决策。

Method: 提出WM-Cov，一个与生成器无关（provider-agnostic）的评估层，将原始提供者输出转换为请求证据、已实现证据和有效证据；通过覆盖率增长、有效失败发现、失败模式多样性、真实性、伪影抑制、重复计数和有效证据精度等维度报告测试充分性。

Result: 在TeraSim/SUMO事件、WM类混合轨迹池和真实DriveArena矩阵（2个规划器×2种时域×6种提示条件×360条ego路线请求）上验证，其中304次尝试成为完全实现证据、56次部分实现；另80条不相交路线切片检查中74条完全实现、6条部分实现。结果表明危险外观事件可能包含有效ADS失败、重复、部分实现和伪影。

Conclusion: 世界模型式测试应依据预算下有效交互证据的收敛性来评估充分性，而非仅依赖原始生成失败或提示覆盖率。

Abstract: World models and generative simulators are emerging as interactive testing infrastructure for autonomous driving because they can react to the ego planner and produce counterfactual, rare, and safety-critical rollouts. This changes a test scenario from a fixed replayed trajectory into an interactive scenario family whose realized evolution depends on the planner under test. The unresolved question is therefore not only whether dangerous rollouts can be generated, but what valid closed-loop evidence is enough to support a specified testing intent and stopping decision. This paper formulates interactive world-model-style testing adequacy and introduces WM-Cov, a provider-agnostic evaluation layer that converts raw provider outputs into requested, realized, and valid evidence. WM-Cov reports adequacy through coverage growth, valid-failure discovery, failure-mode diversity, realism, artifact suppression, duplicate accounting, and valid-evidence precision. Studies on executed TeraSim/SUMO events, WM-like mixed trace pools, and a real DriveArena TrafficManager--WorldDreamer matrix show that dangerous-looking events can include valid ADS failures, duplicates, partial realizations, and artifacts. The DriveArena matrix evaluates two planners, two horizons, six prompt conditions, and 360 ego-route requests; 304 attempts become fully realized evidence and 56 remain partial. A disjoint 80-request route-slice check yields 74 fully realized and 6 partial attempts. The results support evaluating world-model-style testing by convergence of valid interactive evidence under budget, rather than by raw generated failures or prompt coverage alone.

</details>


### [209] [CrystalMem: Elastic Memory for Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Knowledge Crystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00303)
*Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM代理记忆在预算压缩-恢复循环后无法回到原有能力水平（"记忆滞后"）的问题，提出CrystalMem弹性记忆边车，通过四级保真度状态降级与验证式重结晶，在字节和计算预算限制下恢复能力，在七个环境、十七种方法、六个骨干模型上全面最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云平台的配额随负载和成本动态调整，但现有的LLM代理记忆系统假设字节预算只增不减。实际中记忆经历"压缩-恢复"循环后，代理能力会永久性低于压缩前水平（称为记忆滞后）。作者证明任何仅保留或丢弃条目的策略都存在残余亏损下限，因此需要新的记忆管理机制。

Method: 提出CrystalMem（Crystallized Memory），一个弹性记忆边车组件。其核心机制包括：(1) 将记忆条目在四种保真度状态之间降级，遵循"结晶能量"调度；(2) 使用基于优势加权影响力并耦合依赖关系的排序来决定降级顺序；(3) 在显式计算和字节上限约束下，通过验证式重结晶来恢复能力。

Result: 在七个环境、十七种方法、六个骨干模型上，以及多租户服务和物理边缘-云端部署场景下，CrystalMem在所有设置中均取得最高的恢复后能力。在50%字节预算下，CrystalMem在全部环境中匹配最强预算受限基线在全量预算下的表现；在相同预算下平均领先+4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: CrystalMem证明了通过结构化的保真度降级和验证式重结晶机制，可以打破记忆滞后导致的残余能力亏损下限，在弹性资源约束下有效恢复LLM代理的记忆能力，弥补了现有基线方法留下的能力恢复缺口。

Abstract: Memory for self-evolving large language model (LLM) agents is often provisioned as if its byte budget only grows. Cloud platforms, however, adjust quotas with load and cost, and we show that capability does not follow the budget back up: after a squeeze-and-recover cycle, the agent settles below its pre-squeeze level, a gap we call memory hysteresis. The cause is structural. Deletion and one-way compression discard the material needed for later rebuilding, and we prove that any policy that only keeps or drops entries carries a residual-deficit floor. We propose CrystalMem (Crystallized Memory), an elastic memory sidecar that demotes entries across four fidelity states under a crystallization-energy schedule, orders demotions by advantage-weighted influence with dependency coupling, and recovers capability through verified recrystallization under explicit compute and byte caps. Across seven environments, seventeen methods, and six backbones, with multi-tenant serving and a physical edge-cloud deployment, CrystalMem achieves the highest restored capability in every setting and closes the loop left open by every baseline. From a 50% byte budget, CrystalMem matches the strongest budgeted baseline at full provision on every environment; at equal budgets, it leads by +4.6 pp on average.

</details>


### [210] [Trust and Its Betrayal under Three Representational Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00321)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将信任建模为虚拟条件（subjunctive）认知状态的形式化框架，区分能力信任与诚信信任，并考察了三种表示方式（字典序假设、Lewis-Stalnaker球系中的序数接近性、条件概率系统中的强信念）下共同信任与Brandenburger-Keisler不可能性定理的关系，重点分析了共同信任的有限深度与完全不动点的可实现性差异，以及背叛在三种表示下如何以不同方式影响认知结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信任不同于一般的预测性依赖：信任的落空（背叛）在性质上不同于预测的落空。作者希望为这种特殊的命题态度提供一个严谨的认知逻辑基础，并检验在共同信任（而非共同理性假设）下，Brandenburger-Keisler关于共同信念的不可能性定理是否仍然成立。

Method: 作者将信任形式化为两种子junctive认知状态：（1）A信任B的能力：A相信"若P为真，则B会知道P"；（2）A信任B的诚信：A相信"若B知道P，则B会向A披露"。论文在三种表征系统中展开分析：字典序假设、Lewis-Stalnaker球系序数接近性、条件概率系统强信念。针对每种表征，考察共同信任的有限深度（finite depth）与完全不动点（completed common-trust fixed point）性质，并通过一个具体背叛场景检验三种表示的差异。

Result: 三种表示一致认为：共同信任的任意有限深度均可实现，而完全共同信任不动点才是困难的症结所在。但它们在困难如何显现上存在显著分歧：在字典序表示下，一次背叛会推翻主体的层级排序（level ordering）；在序数表示下，背叛会按照背叛者的故意程度污染其接近性排序（closeness ordering）；在强信念表示下，背叛仅会转移其操作条件假设（operative conditioning hypothesis），而信念结构保持自洽。

Conclusion: 不同表示框架对信任与背叛的处理各有优劣：强信念表示似乎最能容纳背叛而不破坏信念的一致性，而字典序和序数表示则更直接地在结构层面反映背叛的冲击。这一对比为理解共同信任的理论极限以及信任与理性假设在认知博弈论中的分野提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Trust is a propositional attitude of a distinctive kind: to trust is to rely on another under conditions where reliance could be disappointed, and the disappointment of trust---betrayal---differs qualitatively from the disappointment of a prediction. We treat trust as a \emph{subjunctive} epistemic state: $A$ trusts $B$'s competence when $A$ believes that \emph{were $P$ true, $B$ would know it}, and $B$'s integrity when $A$ believes that \emph{were $B$ to know $P$, he would disclose it to $A$}. We develop three representations of this state---as lexicographic \emph{assumption} as \emph{ordinal closeness} in a Lewis--Stalnaker sphere system , and as \emph{strong belief} in a conditional probability system and for each we ask whether the Brandenburger--Keisler impossibility on common belief survives when the assumption of rationality is replaced by an assumption of trustworthiness. The three representations agree that every \emph{finite} depth of common trust is realizable while the \emph{completed} common-trust fixed point is the locus of difficulty, but they differ sharply in \emph{how} the difficulty manifests, and---our organizing finding---in how each survives a concrete betrayal. W show that the same betrayal refutes an agent's \emph{level ordering} under the lexicographic representation, contaminates her \emph{closeness ordering} in proportion to the betrayer's deliberateness under the ordinal representation, and merely \emph{shifts her operative conditioning hypothesis} while leaving her belief structure coherent under the strong-belief representation.

</details>


### [211] [Learning to Coordinate Symbolic Tools: LLM Agents for Verified Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00326)
*Bohan Chen,Shivam N. Patel,Richard Hoffmann,Sam Looi,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）通过工具调用解决加权平方和（SOS）分解问题，提出了一种结合代数任务训练、符号工具（SymPy）和基于验证器反馈的强化学习（GRPO）的智能体系统。在合成数据集上，该系统在加权SOS分解上达到78.96%的验证成功率和91.75%的宏平均准确率，显著优于基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用使LLM能够在问题求解中调用外部计算，在AI4Math等领域具有重要价值。加权SOS分解是证明多项式非负性的可机检验证途径，但候选分解的寻找需要处理非唯一的重新分组和协调多种符号变换，对LLM构成挑战，因此需要开发专门的智能体系统。

Method: （1）构建135万个合成示例，覆盖八个支撑性多项式任务和加权SOS分解任务；（2）使用监督微调（SFT）训练代数问题和模拟符号轨迹；（3）采用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）结合任务特定的符号奖励进行强化学习；（4）在评估阶段使用原生SymPy调用进行展开、收集、重排和分解操作；（5）每个最终SOS答案通过精确展开和系数比较进行验证。

Result: 在相同生成器的合成测试集上，完整的SFT+GRPO+工具系统在四种配置中表现最强：加权SOS验证成功率78.96%（对比基座模型+工具的44.73%），九个多项式任务的宏平均准确率达91.75%。

Conclusion: 该工作为在输出可精确验证的领域中设计工具调用智能体提供了案例研究，表明结合领域特定技能训练、可执行工具和验证器反馈的三方策略能够有效提升LLM在数学推理任务上的表现，可为其他类似领域提供设计参考。

Abstract: Tool calling allows large language models (LLMs) to invoke external computation during problem solving, a useful capability in various fields including AI for mathematics. We study this setting through weighted sum-of-squares (SOS) decomposition, a machine-checkable route to proving polynomial nonnegativity and hence polynomial inequalities. A candidate decomposition can be checked exactly, but finding one requires choosing among non-unique regroupings and coordinating multiple symbolic transformations. We develop an agent that combines algebraic task training, symbolic tools, and verifier-grounded optimization for this task. Rather than training only on the composite SOS task, we construct 1.35 million synthetic examples covering eight supporting polynomial tasks together with weighted-SOS decomposition. We first apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to direct algebra problems and simulated symbolic traces, and then use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific symbolic rewards. The SFT corpus contains no native tool-calling messages; at evaluation, the agent uses native SymPy calls for expansion, collection, reordering, and factorization. Every final SOS answer is checked by exact expansion and coefficient comparison. On held-out, same-generator synthetic problems, the full SFT+GRPO+tools system is the strongest of four evaluated configurations, reaching 78.96% verified success on weighted SOS, compared with 44.73% for the base model with the same tools, and 91.75% macro accuracy across nine polynomial tasks. Within this controlled setting, our work provides a case study of combining domain-specific skill training, executable tools, and verifier feedback, and may inform the design of tool-calling agents in other domains with exactly checkable outputs.

</details>


### [212] [RMSWeb: Reflection, Failure-Mode Mining, and Salvage-DS for Web Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00335)
*Chengbo Liu,Lifang Zhou,Ruijie Yan,Pei Tan,Ao Sun,Haojun Huang,Guichun Hua,Sining Wei,Yining Chen,Yingying He,Yutao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RMSWeb，一个面向紧凑型Web智能体的三部分训练配方（反思条件重试、失败模式挖掘和Salvage-DS奖励机制），在Qwen3-VL-Instruct 8B/32B上显着提升Web任务性能，同时减少动作步数、降低部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型Web智能体能降低部署成本，但训练面临两大挑战：(1) 成功轨迹收集昂贵且常含低效绕路；(2) SFT后强化学习阶段，全轨迹语料被常规状态主导，且组相对RL在设计不当的动作级奖励下会产生微弱或误导的相对更新，被判定不适合更新的组无法获得回退学习信号。

Method: RMSWeb包含三部分：(1) 反射条件重试（Reflection-conditioned retries）：增加数据收集产量并缩短成功轨迹；(2) 失败模式挖掘（Failure-mode mining）：将离线RL聚焦于SFT策略暴露的关键失败状态；(3) Salvage-DS：结合动作语义极化奖励（action-semantic polarized reward）、对比能力门控动态采样（contrast-and-competence-gated dynamic sampling），以及为被拒绝组提供动作锚点（action-only anchor）的救回机制。

Result: 反射收集数据训练的策略在已解决任务上减少多达19.7%的动作步骤。在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和WebTailBench上，RMSWeb相比SFT在8B上提升2.4-7.0个百分点，在32B上提升1.2-7.7个百分点。8B模型在Online-Mind2Web上达到相似规模开源模型中的最强结果，并在WebVoyager和WebTailBench上取得领先的准确率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: RMSWeb通过系统性的数据收集增强、失败状态聚焦和精细化的奖励/采样设计，有效解决了紧凑Web智能体在SFT后RL训练中的数据稀缺和奖励信号质量问题，在不增加模型规模的前提下实现了性能提升和成本降低。

Abstract: Compact web agents can reduce deployment cost, but training them poses challenges in both data collection and post-SFT reinforcement learning (RL). Successful trajectories are expensive to collect and often contain inefficient detours. After supervised fine-tuning (SFT), full trajectory corpora are dominated by routine states; moreover, when group-relative RL is applied to web actions, inadequately designed action-level rewards can yield weak or misleading relative updates, while groups rejected as unsuitable for such updates receive no fallback learning signal. We present RMSWeb, a three-part recipe for Qwen3-VL-Instruct at 8B and 32B. Reflection-conditioned retries increase collection yield and shorten successful trajectories; failure-mode mining concentrates offline RL on critical states exposed by the SFT policy; and Salvage-DS combines an action-semantic polarized reward, contrast-and-competence-gated dynamic sampling, and an action-only anchor for rejected groups. Policies trained with reflection-collected data use up to 19.7% fewer action steps on solved tasks. On WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and WebTailBench, RMSWeb improves over SFT by 2.4-7.0 points at 8B and 1.2-7.7 points at 32B. Our 8B model also achieves the strongest reported Online-Mind2Web result among similarly sized open-weight models in our comparison and a leading reported accuracy-cost trade-off on WebVoyager and WebTailBench, with the caveat that external evaluation protocols differ.

</details>


### [213] [Bayesian and Motivated Reasoning in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00339)
*Eddie Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明AI智能体在面对相同的数值数据时，会因情景框架（framing）的不同而得出不同的结论。在医学、选举法证和地缘政治预测等高风险领域进行的12项智能体-领域对比实验中，智能体的结论受其先验信念的强烈影响：当结论与先验一致时更倾向于肯定，反之则更否定。框架还改变了智能体的工作方式——包括搜索范围、分析规格选择和证据评估。这揭示了将决策委托给AI智能体的特定风险，因为其决策可能依赖于任务中未指定、决策记录中不可见的先验信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地在开放任务中执行并指导关键决策，但人们对它们是否真正"客观"地处理数据缺乏系统性认识。作者旨在检验一个核心问题：当证据完全相同但情景框架改变时，AI智能体是否会得出不同结论？这是评估AI系统可靠性和潜在偏见的关键前提。

Method: 作者采用"保持证据不变、改变框架"的对照实验设计：在医学、选举法证和地缘政治预测三个高风险领域，将同一组数值数据嵌入不同场景中呈现给不同的AI智能体，共进行12个智能体-领域组合的对比。通过比较智能体在框架与先验一致/冲突两种情况下的结论倾向，以及分析其搜索行为、分析规格选择和证据评估方式的变化来检验框架效应。

Result: 实验结果显示，AI智能体的结论显著受其先验信念影响：当结论围绕其认为可能成立的主张构建时，智能体更倾向于得出肯定结论；当框架与其先验冲突时则相反。此外，框架改变了智能体的工作过程——搜索更广泛、选择不同的分析规格、对相同证据的评价也不同。这些效应在12个智能体-领域比较中均稳定出现。

Conclusion: AI智能体并非纯粹的"理性数据分析器"，其推理过程受框架效应和先验信念的系统性影响。这构成将关键决策委托给AI智能体的特定风险：智能体的结论可能取决于未被任务指定、也不在决策记录中可见的先验信念，导致决策看似基于数据但实际上被隐性偏见所驱动，且这种偏见难以被审计和发现。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform open-ended tasks in settings where their conclusions can guide consequential decisions. We provide evidence that AI agents draw different conclusions from identical numerical data when the substantive framing changes. We demonstrate this behavior in high-stakes domains in medicine, election forensics, and geopolitical forecasting by holding the evidence fixed while changing the scenario in which the evidence appears. Across twelve agent-domain comparisons, agents' conclusions are strongly influenced by their prior beliefs. They are more likely to reach an affirmative conclusion when it is framed around a proposition they already regard as likely, while the reverse holds when the framing conflicts with their prior. The framing also changes how some agents work: they search more extensively, choose different analytical specifications, and evaluate the same evidence differently. These results identify a particular risk of delegating decision-making to AI agents, as their decisions may depend on prior beliefs that are neither specified in the task nor visible in the decision record.

</details>


### [214] [Gene Ontology-Guided Hierarchical Spatial Gene Expression Prediction from Histopathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00405)
*Zhiwen Xu,Xiaoming Yan,Chengkun Wu,Juan Chen,Haoang Chi,Liyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSGR（多尺度基因细化器），利用基因本体（GO）作为结构化先验，将目标基因组织成四层GO树，通过GO引导解码器以残差校正方式从粗粒度功能域逐步细化到细粒度单基因预测，提升空间基因表达预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从组织病理学图像预测空间基因表达的方法将目标基因集视为扁平、无结构的向量，忽略了基因间因共享生物通路和调控程序而存在的相互依赖关系。缺乏显式结构引导时，模型只能从有限的配对数据中推断这些依赖关系，限制了预测质量。

Method: MSGR将基因本体（GO）——一个基因功能层级体系——作为显式结构先验，把目标基因组织为四层GO树。其中GO引导解码器在尺度加权监督下，通过残差校正从粗粒度功能域逐步细化预测到细粒度单基因。该解码器仅作用于基因侧，可作为即插即用模块无缝替换现有架构，无需修改图像侧。

Result: 在HEST-1k基准的九个数据集上的大量实验验证了两个核心论断：GO结构化解码始终优于扁平解码（甚至超越最先进的生成式基线）；收益归因于生物学本体结构而非层级分解本身，与结构等价的随机层级相比取得了+0.027的边际优势。

Conclusion: 实验证明将基因本体结构作为显式先验引入解码过程能有效提升空间基因表达预测精度，且该改进源于生物学语义结构而非泛化的层级机制，MSGR作为通用即插即用组件具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from histopathology images enables large-scale transcriptomic profiling without the cost of direct measurement. Existing methods decode the target gene set as a flat, unstructured vector, ignoring the inter-gene dependencies arising from shared biological pathways and regulatory programs. Without explicit structural guidance, models must infer these dependencies entirely from limited paired data, constraining prediction quality. We propose MSGR (Multi-Scale Gene Refiner), which bridges this gap by incorporating the Gene Ontology (GO), a curated functional hierarchy of genes, as an explicit structural prior. MSGR organizes target genes into a four-level GO tree. Its GO-guided decoder then progressively refines predictions from coarse functional domains to fine individual genes via residual corrections under scale-weighted supervision. Operating solely on the gene side, the GO-guided decoder serves as a seamless plug-in replacement that consistently improves existing architectures without requiring any image-side modifications. Extensive experiments on nine datasets from the HEST-1k benchmark provide empirical evidence for two central claims: GO-structured decoding consistently outperforms flat decoding, even against a state-of-the-art generative baseline, and the gain is attributable to biological ontology structure rather than hierarchical decomposition per se, as confirmed by a +0.027 margin over a structurally equivalent random hierarchy.

</details>


### [215] [Where did the ambiguity go? Examining how multimodal models interpret polysemous words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00410)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Raja Marjieh,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多义词在文本生成和图像生成模型中的多义性表达差异。通过对17个文生图和15个文本生成模型进行测试，发现图像生成模型生成的多义词语义多样性远低于文本生成模型（归一化熵0.10 vs 0.25），且两者均远低于人类想象的多义性（0.47）。此外，模型对自身输出分布的预测比实际输出更多样，揭示了基础模型在不同模态间表达意义的"多模态鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类语言具有高度多义性，许多常见词汇有多种含义。虽然已有研究表明大型语言模型能理解这种多义性，但多义性在图像等其他模态中如何呈现尚不清楚，这促使作者跨模态研究基础模型对多义词的不同表达方式。

Method: 作者选取多义词，分别输入17个文生图模型和15个文本生成模型，在无上下文限定含义的情况下多次采样，测量生成输出中出现的不同词义。使用归一化熵衡量语义多样性，并进一步让模型自我预测其输出分布。

Result: 发现多模态鸿沟显著：所有模型家族内，图像生成的语义多样性远低于文本生成（归一化熵0.10 vs 0.25），二者都远低于人类想象（0.47）。同时模型对自身"将生成什么"的预测分布比实际输出更为多样。

Conclusion: 基础模型在不同模态间对多义词的表达能力存在显著差异，其词汇理解不能在不同模态间忠实且均等地迁移，揭示了跨模态语义表达的一个系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Human language is highly polysemous. Many common words (e.g., 'bank' or 'palm') carry several distinct meanings that shape what humans communicate and imagine. Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to understand this multiplicity of meaning, but much less is known about how polysemy surfaces in other modalities such as images. We study this across 17 text-to-image and 15 text-generation models by giving each a polysemous word with no context to fix its meaning and measuring which senses are produced over many samples. We find a clear multimodal gap, where within every model family, generated images settle on far fewer senses than generated sentences (normalized entropy 0.10 vs. 0.25), and both are far less varied than what people imagine for the same words (normalized entropy 0.47). However, when we instead ask a model to list how often it would generate outputs corresponding to each possible meaning of a word, it predicts distributions that are more diverse than the actual space of outputs. These results reveal a multimodal gap in how foundation models express meaning, and how their understanding may not transfer faithfully nor equally across modalities.

</details>


### [216] [SymboUQ: Symbolic Uncertainty Quantification for Spatial Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00417)
*Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymboUQ是一个符号化不确定性量化框架，通过区分可符号化性（claim能否在验证器形式语言中表示）与语义确定性（执行是否产生明确判定结果），从推理轨迹中估计大语言模型（LLM）最终答案的可靠性。在五个空间推理基准和四个冻结LLM骨干网络上，相对最强基线实现了约8%的AUROC提升和7%的类平衡Brier损失的相对降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的中间空间推理轨迹虽然流畅，但其关系可能在逻辑上无法支撑最终结论，导致基于token级置信度的可靠性估计不足。现有形式验证器能提供更强的语义证据，但其适用性不完整：解析出的声明不一定能产生确定的语义判定（可能返回unknown或not-evaluable）。因此需要一种能区分不同不确定性来源并据此估计答案可靠性的框架。

Method: SymboUQ包含三个核心组件：(i) Layout Auditor——执行有序空间声明并提取可行性、冲突和修复证据；(ii) 无标签的Determinacy Profile——刻画有效的可执行覆盖率；(iii) Determinacy-Aware Reliability Composer——根据验证器适用性整合基于约束、基于表示和基于解码的分数。核心思想是将不确定性分为两个维度：符号化性（声明能否形式化表示）和语义确定性（执行能否产生明确判定）。

Result: 在五个空间推理基准上，使用四个冻结LLM骨干网络进行实验，SymboUQ相对最强基线在AUROC上实现了约8%的相对改进，在类平衡Brier损失上实现了约7%的相对降低。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性量化从token级置信度扩展为符号验证驱动的双向可解释性分析（区分可符号化性与语义确定性），SymboUQ有效提升了空间推理任务中LLM最终答案可靠性的估计精度，为符号验证器部分适用场景下的可靠性建模提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) can produce fluent spatial reasoning traces, their intermediate relations may fail to support the final conclusion, making token-level confidence insufficient for final-answer reliability estimation. Existing formal verifiers provide stronger semantic evidence, but their applicability is partial: a parsed claim need not yield a definite semantic verdict. To address this issue, we introduce SymboUQ, a symbolic uncertainty quantification framework that estimates final-answer reliability from reasoning traces by distinguishing symbolizability, whether a claim can be represented in the verifier's formal language, from semantic determinacy, whether its execution yields an entailed or contradicted verdict rather than an unknown or not-evaluable outcome. SymboUQ comprises (i) a Layout Auditor that executes ordered spatial claims and extracts feasibility, conflict, and repair evidence; (ii) a label-free Determinacy Profile that characterizes effective executable coverage; and (iii) a Determinacy-Aware Reliability Composer that integrates constraint-based, representation-based, and decoding-based scores according to verifier applicability. Extensive experiments on five spatial reasoning benchmarks with four frozen LLM backbones show that SymboUQ achieves approximately an 8% relative improvement in AUROC and a 7% relative reduction in class-balanced Brier loss over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [217] [Mask-Based Priors Are More Persistent than Query-Key Initializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00418)
*Mingze Ma,Hemanth Saratchandran,Cameron Gordon,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用可学习的注意力掩码（attention mask）初始化来替代现有的QK投影结构化初始化方法，从而为Transformer注入持久的归纳偏置。在布尔外推任务上，掩码初始化能实现近乎完美的外推，而vanilla和QK初始化方法仍受限于默认的简化插值偏差。该方法在低数据算术、视觉和语言基准上也有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究表明Transformer在未见数据上并非仅仅缺少数据，而是以系统性的方式错误泛化——倾向于学习最小度插值器而非真实目标函数。现有通过初始化QK投影注入结构先验的方法在布尔外推任务中会被训练过程快速覆盖，无法改变学习到的外推规则。因此需要一种更持久、更直接的结构先验注入方式。

Method: 提出直接用可学习的加性注意力掩码进行初始化。该掩码是有限的、可学习的注意力对数偏置（attention-logit bias），由任务层面的交互结构初始化而来。与已有的QK投影初始化不同，掩码将结构先验与内容相关的注意力分数解耦，使其在优化过程中能够持续存在，不受训练覆盖。

Result: 在布尔推理任务上，掩码初始化实现接近完美的外推性能，而vanilla和QK初始化的Transformer仍被困于默认的归纳偏差。该方法还提升了低数据下的算术性能，在视觉和语言基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 注意力掩码不仅可作为架构约束，还能作为编码持久归纳偏置的简单媒介。通过将结构先验与内容相关注意力分数分离，掩码初始化提供了一种纠正Transformer系统性错误泛化的有效手段。

Abstract: Transformers do not merely lack data on some Boolean extrapolation tasks; they generalize in a systematically wrong way. Recent work on generalization on the unseen has shown that, despite fitting the observed domain, Transformers often extrapolate according to a simpler minimum-degree interpolator rather than the true target function. These Boolean tasks are not practical applications, but controlled stress tests for understanding Transformer inductive bias. We ask whether this failure mode can be corrected by injecting explicit structural priors into attention. Existing structured-initialization methods alter Transformer inductive bias indirectly, by choosing query and key projections whose similarity scores approximate a desired attention pattern. However, we find that when applied to Boolean extrapolation, these QK-based priors can be rapidly overwritten during training and fail to change the learned extrapolation rule. We propose a simpler alternative: initialize the additive attention mask directly. Unlike standard hard masks used for causality or locality attention, our mask is a finite, learnable attention-logit bias initialized from task-level interaction structure. This separates the structural prior from content-dependent attention scores, allowing it to persist throughout optimization. On Boolean reasoning tasks, mask-based initialization achieves near-perfect extrapolation where vanilla and QK-initialized Transformers remain trapped by the default inductive bias. The same mechanism also improves low-data arithmetic performance and remains competitive on vision and language benchmarks. These results show that attention masks can serve not only as architectural constraints, but as a simple substrate for encoding persistent inductive bias in Transformers.

</details>


### [218] [TrAC: Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency for Efficient Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00422)
*Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrAC（Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency），一种基于正确性监督的不确定性量化框架，通过主动重提取（PCE）和被动轨迹不确定性轮廓（TUP）相结合，在单条完整推理轨迹上实现更可靠的响应正确性估计。在五个数学推理基准和三个LLM家族上，TrAC相比八样本自一致性提升了1.8%的macro AUROC，并降低了3.4%的AURC，且仅需一条完整推理轨迹和短缓存答案探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性估计方法可分为三类：被动单轨迹方法（使用token级置信度信号）、基于采样的方法（比较多条完整轨迹但生成成本高）、以及基于前缀的主动方法（探测部分轨迹）。然而，现有方法均未主动从已完成的推理轨迹中重新引出答案，以衡量其与原始答案的一致性和支持度。这一空白促使作者提出主动重提取机制来填补。

Method: TrAC由两个核心组件构成：（1）主动组件Prefix-Conditioned Elicitation (PCE)，以完整推理轨迹为条件重新引出简短答案，同时表示该答案与原始答案的一致性及其token级概率支持；（2）被动组件Trace Uncertainty Profile (TUP)，在无需额外解码的情况下汇总原始生成过程中token级不确定性的演变。两个表示通过一个轻量级头部网络集成，输出响应正确性分数。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准和三个LLM家族上，TrAC相比八样本自一致性将macro AUROC提升1.8%，并将AURC降低3.4%，同时仅使用一条完整推理轨迹和一个短缓存答案探针。当八条样本已可用时，将重提取与样本共识结合可进一步提升macro AUROC 4.3%，并降低AURC 8.3%，且无需额外生成完整轨迹。

Conclusion: TrAC验证了主动重提取信号在响应级不确定性估计中的有效性，显示在更低生成成本下可获得优于多采样自一致性的性能，且与现有采样方法具有正交互补性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent reasoning traces that nevertheless lead to incorrect answers, making response-level uncertainty estimation important for abstention, human review, and adaptive compute allocation. Existing approaches generally fall into three categories: passive single-trace methods use token-level confidence signals, sampling-based methods compare multiple complete traces at higher generation cost, and active prefix-based methods probe partial traces to study answer stabilization or preference transitions. However, none actively re-elicits an answer from a completed reasoning trace to measure its consistency with and support for the original answer. To address this gap, we introduce Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency (TrAC), a correctness-supervised uncertainty quantification framework that combines active and passive signals anchored to one completed reasoning trace. Its active component, Prefix-Conditioned Elicitation (PCE), re-elicits a short answer conditioned on the completed trace and represents both its consistency with the original answer and its token-level probabilistic support. Its passive component, Trace Uncertainty Profile (TUP), summarizes how token-level uncertainty evolves throughout the original generation without additional decoding. A lightweight head then integrates the two representations into a response-correctness score. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three LLM families, TrAC improves macro AUROC by 1.8% and reduces AURC by 3.4% relative to eight-sample self-consistency, while using one complete reasoning trace and a short cached answer probe. When eight samples are already available, augmenting sample consensus with re-elicitation further improves macro AUROC by 4.3% and reduces AURC by 8.3%, without additional full-trace generation.

</details>


### [219] [Diagnose Before You Compress: Prediction-Independent Bottleneck Witness Refinement for LLM Serving Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00423)
*Liming Liu,Chao Hu,Mingfei Lu,Cong Tan,Yiwei Ge,Chijin Zhou,Yongjun Xie,Runzhe Wang,Xiaohai Shi,Heyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BPW是一个面向LLM服务回放套件的质量约束框架，通过工作负载候选提名、覆盖优先序列构建和瓶颈真值验证三阶段，生成紧凑且诊断可靠的测试套件，在保持证据覆盖所有关键瓶颈组件的同时显著优于16种基线策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM服务于生成海量多样化请求，全量轨迹回放成本高昂。现有轨迹缩减方法主要保留工作负载分布或代表性请求，但揭示瓶颈的工作负载可能稀缺且不具备代表性；同时单个组件的证据无法弥补其他组件的证据缺失，而用预测瓶颈作为真值会产生循环评估。因此需要为每个瓶颈组件保留证据，而非仅仅依赖工作负载代表性。

Method: BPW包含三个阶段：(1) 工作负载候选提名（Workload Candidate Nomination）：使用响应无关的工作负载特征和闭源侧测量，识别可能暴露调度器、预填充、解码或KV缓存瓶颈的工作负载；(2) 覆盖优先序列构建（Coverage-Priority Sequence Construction）：将多组件提案组织为可复用的超边，优先处理薄弱和未覆盖的维度；(3) 瓶颈真值验证（Bottleneck Truth Verification）：仅通过直接目标系统测量推导与预测无关的标签，确定满足每个组件双证人要求的最早前缀。

Result: 在BurstGPT、ServeGen和Mooncake数据集上，BPW以紧凑的工作负载集达到验证门槛，优于16种策略，Mean前缀Macro-F1和WBRC-AUC分别相对提升2.3%和16.3%。阶段消融和敏感性分析证实了三阶段的独特贡献和局部稳定性。

Conclusion: BPW证明通过质量约束的框架，可以不依赖工作负载代表性而直接为每个瓶颈组件保留诊断证据，构建既紧凑又诊断可靠的LLM服务回放套件，代码已开源。

Abstract: Production LLM serving generates millions of diverse requests, making full-trace replay across serving configurations increasingly expensive. Existing trace reduction methods mainly preserve workload distributions or representative requests, but bottleneck-revealing workloads may be rare and non-representative. Moreover, evidence for one component cannot compensate for missing evidence in another, while using predicted bottlenecks as target truth creates circular evaluation. These limitations make it necessary to preserve evidence for every bottleneck component rather than rely on workload representativeness alone. We propose Bottleneck-Preserving Witnessing (BPW), a quality-constrained framework for compact and diagnostically reliable LLM serving replay suites. BPW first performs Workload Candidate Nomination using response-blind workload features and closed source-side measurements. This stage identifies workloads that may expose scheduler, prefill, decode, or KV-cache bottlenecks. Coverage-Priority Sequence Construction then organizes multi-component proposals as reusable hyperedges and prioritizes weak and uncovered dimensions. Finally, Bottleneck Truth Verification derives prediction-independent labels solely from direct target-system measurements. The verified results determine the earliest prefix satisfying the direct two-witness requirement for every component. Experiments on BurstGPT, ServeGen, and Mooncake show that BPW reaches the verified gate with a compact workload set and outperforms 16 policies, achieving relative improvements of 2.3% and 16.3% in Mean prefix Macro-F1 and WBRC-AUC, respectively. Stage-resolved and sensitivity analyses confirm the distinct contributions and local stability of its three stages. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/llmllmllm/BPW

</details>


### [220] [Ekova: A Personality-Support Agent for Self-Discovery Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00478)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出人格支持（PS）这一新范式，区别于传统情绪支持（ES），旨在帮助用户更清晰地认识自我、提升认知清晰度与自我表达能力。作者构建了包含8,590个样本的中文自我发现数据集DSD，开发了多人格系统的DeepSupport模型（采用OrthoTune训练框架，含风格特定适配器和风格一致性正则化器），并将其统一为带跨会话记忆层的持久性人格支持智能体Ekova。实验表明OrthoTune训练的模型在所有指标上平均相对提升16.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情绪支持（ES）系统长期以来只优化单一目标——即时缓解用户的情绪困扰。作者认为存在一个互补但被忽视的需求：帮助用户更清晰地认识自己，这构成了一个全新的范式（人格支持PS），需要从数据到模型到系统架构的全方位构建。

Method: 方法包含三个层次：（1）构建DSD中文自我发现数据集，含8,590条真实纵向交互样本，覆盖五种最小单元风格（Coach、Warm、Tsukkomi、Real、Gonzo）；（2）开发DeepSupport多人格系统，采用OrthoTune训练框架，包含风格特定适配器（style-specific adapters）与风格一致性正则化器（style-consistency regularizer）；（3）将五种DeepSupport人格统一为Ekova持久性人格支持智能体，配备统一的跨会话记忆层，支持自适应路由与用户自定义人格选择。

Result: 实验结果表明，OrthoTune训练的模型在所有指标上优于所有基线，相较于最强的基于提示（prompt-based）的基线，平均相对增益达16.3%。代码已开源在GitHub。

Conclusion: 人格支持（PS）是与情绪支持（ES）互补且独立的研究范式，专注于认知清晰度与自我表达而非症状缓解或诊断。通过DSD数据集、DeepSupport/OrthoTune训练框架以及Ekova智能体的三层架构，该工作证明这一范式在技术和经验层面都具备可行性。

Abstract: Emotional Support (ES) systems have long optimized a single objective: alleviating the user's emotional distress in the moment. We argue that a complementary need, helping users see themselves more clearly, defines a distinct paradigm we call Personality Support (PS). PS is not counseling or clinical intervention: it targets cognitive clarity and self-articulation, not symptom relief or diagnosis. We instantiate this paradigm in three layers. First, we present DSD, a Chinese self-discovery PS Dataset of 8,590 samples collected through real longitudinal interaction across five minimal units, Coach, Warm, Tsukkomi, Real, and Gonzo. Second, we build DeepSupport, a multi-persona PS system trained with OrthoTune, a PS-tailored framework with style-specific adapters and a style-consistency regularizer. Third, we unify the five DeepSupport personas into Ekova, a persistent personality-support agent with a unified cross-session memory layer, supporting both adaptive routing and user-customized persona selection. Experiments show that OrthoTune-trained models outperform all baselines with an average relative gain of 16.3% across all metrics over the strongest prompt-based baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Yukyin/Ekova.

</details>


### [221] [F-WANDA: Fisher-Reweighted Post-Training Pruning for Sustainable Deployment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00481)
*Himanshu Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: F-WANDA是一种单次后训练剪枝方法，通过重新分配每行保留预算（基于预激活的经验Fisher信息）来改进WANDA，在保持WANDA高效（仅需一次额外反向传播）的同时达到接近SPARSEGPT的质量，在LLAMA-2-7B 50%非结构化稀疏度下实现WikiText-2困惑度6.85，5-shot MMLU分别比WANDA和SPARSEGPT高1.6pp和1.1pp，且剪枝耗时和能耗仅为SPARSEGPT的三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM单次后训练剪枝方法存在权衡困境：WANDA计算高效但剪枝质量有限，SPARSEGPT质量较好但计算成本高昂。作者旨在开发一种既高效又高质量的去中心化剪枝方法，以实现可持续的LLM压缩。

Method: F-WANDA是WANDA的直接修改方案，核心创新在于将每行保留预算按预激活的经验Fisher信息比例在输出神经元间重新分配。Fisher信息通过一次额外的反向传播在WANDA已使用的同一校准语料上收集，不更新任何权重，因此保留了单次剪枝的极低计算开销。

Result: 在LLAMA-2-7B 50%非结构化稀疏度下，F-WANDA取得WikiText-2困惑度6.85；5-shot MMLU较WANDA提升1.6个百分点，较SPARSEGPT提升1.1个百分点；剪枝墙钟时间和能耗仅为SPARSEGPT的三分之一；流利度与WANDA相当。

Conclusion: F-WANDA在不额外增加校准数据或微调的情况下达到了质量与剪枝成本的帕累托前沿，为可持续的LLM压缩提供了一种兼顾质量与效率的单次后训练剪枝方案。

Abstract: One-shot post-training pruning is the most energy-frugal compression strategy for largelanguage models (LLMs), yet existing approaches trade either quality (WANDA) or compute cost (SPARSEGPT). We introduce F-WANDA, a drop-in modification of WANDA that reallocates the per-row keep budget across output neurons in proportion to the empirical Fisher information of the pre-activation. The Fisher signal is collected in a single additional backward pass over the same calibration corpus WANDA already uses; no weights are updated. On LLAMA-2-7B at 50 % unstructured sparsity, F-WANDA attains WikiText-2 perplexity of 6.85, matches WANDA fluency, and improves 5-shot MMLU by +1.6 pp over WANDA and +1.1 pp over SPARSEGPT, while incurring only one-third of SPARSEGPT pruning wall-clock and energy. The headline trade-off is achieved without extra calibration data or fine-tuning, placing F-WANDA on the Pareto frontier of quality versus pruning cost for sustainable LLM compression.

</details>


### [222] [The Bayesian Reflex: A Predictive Coding Engine for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00492)
*Sourabh Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"贝叶斯反射"框架，通过三个支柱（层级生成模型的信念维护、基于预测误差最小化的序贯贝叶斯更新、基于主动推断的不确定性驱动行动）直接实例化预测编码理论，并利用椭球分解、递归高斯过程和导数感知贝叶斯优化等近期突破作为算法基石，实现数学上严谨、可扩展且受大脑启发的持续学习、感知与决策，在气候模型评估和质数发现等应用中展示了适应性人工智能的蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码是解释大脑皮层计算的强大理论，但一直缺乏相应的可扩展算法实现，难以直接转化为人工智能的实际应用。

Method: 提出"贝叶斯反射"计算框架，由三个支柱构成：①通过层级生成模型进行信念维护；②通过预测误差最小化进行序贯贝叶斯更新；③通过主动推断实现不确定性驱动的行动。框架核心算法组件包括：用于精确i.i.d.采样的椭球分解、用于深度层级推断的递归高斯过程、以及导数感知的贝叶斯优化。

Result: 该框架实现了数学上严谨、可扩展且受大脑启发的持续学习、感知与决策能力，并在气候模型评估与质数发现等多个跨领域应用中展示了其通用性与适应性。

Conclusion: 该研究为预测编码理论从认知科学到可部署AI系统之间的桥梁提供了具体可行的算法蓝图，预示了真正自适应人工智能的实现路径。

Abstract: Predictive coding offers a powerful theory of cortical computation, but corresponding scalable algorithmic implementations for artificial intelligence have remained elusive. This paper introduces the Bayesian reflex, a computational framework that directly instantiates predictive coding through three pillars: belief maintenance via hierarchical generative models, sequential Bayesian updating via prediction-error minimization, and uncertainty-driven action via active inference. We show that recent breakthroughs---ellipsoidal decomposition for exact $i.i.d.$ sampling, recursive Gaussian processes for deep hierarchical inference, and derivative-aware Bayesian optimization---provide the missing algorithmic ingredients. The resulting framework enables mathematically principled, scalable, and brain-inspired continual learning, perception, and decision-making. We illustrate its versatility through applications ranging from climate model evaluation to prime number discovery, offering a blueprint for truly adaptive artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [223] [TaPR: Test-Aware Policy Refinement for Feedback-Conditioned Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00494)
*Aofan Liu,Jingxiang Meng,Fangxin Liu,Yongbiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Test-aware Policy Refinement (TaPR)框架，将多轮代码智能体的执行反馈转化为密集的逐轮测试通过率奖励，在统一的多轮交互协议下进行策略优化。在六个模型、219个LiveCodeBench问题上的实验显示，TaPR将三轮成功率和Pass@3提高2.44个百分点，并在7B/8B高头部空间子集上从30.25%提升至33.56%。该工作的核心贡献在于奖励分解框架和轮次感知评估协议，用于解耦首轮生成质量与多轮修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习范式主要使用单轮结果奖励来优化策略，这与多轮代码智能体依赖执行反馈进行程序修复的实际交互模式不一致。这种错位混淆了初始代码生成与反馈驱动的精修过程，丢弃了中间轮次的细粒度执行信号，且无法评估策略是否真正获得了自我修复能力。

Method: TaPR提出将执行反馈转化为密集的逐轮测试通过率（test-pass-ratio）奖励，并在一致的多轮交互协议下进行策略训练。该方法对每一轮的测试通过比例进行奖励分配，提供比单轮结果奖励更细粒度的学习信号。

Result: 在六个模型和219个代码生成问题（LiveCodeBench）上，TaPR将三轮成功率Pass@3平均提升2.44个百分点。在预定义的7B/8B高头部空间子集上，平均准确率从30.25%提升至33.56%（+3.31 pp），配对实验中42次改进、13次回退。在匹配的Qwen3-8B消融实验中，密集奖励在前十步均提供非零反馈，并在测试预算内达到比仅结果奖励GRPO更高的Hard子集峰值，但GRPO在步数300时几乎追平Pooled Pass@3。

Conclusion: 本工作的主要贡献是一个奖励分解框架和一个轮次感知的评估协议，能够将首轮生成质量与多轮修复能力进行解耦，从而更准确地衡量并优化代码智能体的自我修复能力。

Abstract: Multi-turn code agents rely on execution feedback to repair incorrect programs, yet standard reinforcement learning paradigms optimize and evaluate policy performance primarily using single-shot outcome rewards. This misalignment conflates initial code generation with feedback-driven refinement, discards granular execution signals across intermediate turns, and fails to evaluate whether the policy actually acquires self-repair capabilities. We propose Test-aware Policy Refinement (TaPR), a framework that transforms execution feedback into a dense per-turn test-pass-ratio reward under a consistent multi-turn interaction protocol. Across six models on 219 code-generation problems from LiveCodeBench, TaPR improves the pooled three-turn success rate (Pass@3) by 2.44 percentage points. In the predefined 7B/8B high-headroom slice, pooled accuracy increases from 30.25% to 33.56% (+3.31 pp), with 42 improvements and 13 regressions in paired trials. On a matched Qwen3-8B ablation, the dense reward supplies nonzero feedback in all of the first ten steps and reaches a higher Hard-subset peak than outcome-only GRPO within the tested budget, although GRPO nearly matches pooled Pass@3 by step 300. Our primary contribution is a reward-decomposition framework and a turn-aware evaluation protocol that decouple first-shot generation quality from multi-turn repair competence.

</details>


### [224] [BayesSeg: A Bayesian Optimization Framework for State Segmentation of Electricity Consumption Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00513)
*Zhenya Zhang,Wendi Zhu,Ping Wang,Hongmei Cheng,Shuguang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesSeg，一个统一的非侵入式负荷监测(NILM)时间序列自适应分割框架，集成分割、多维评估和自动参数优化三大层。基于双稳态准则和互补集解析实现无监督精确分割，结合事件级F1分数与归一化互信息(NMI)构建复合评估指标，并通过贝叶斯优化(TPE代理模型)实现参数空间的高效探索。在SustDataED2数据集上仅需约100次目标评估即可达到与网格搜索仅0.35%偏差的结果，加权复合分数达0.7149，event_F1达0.9340，优化延迟从约5300秒降至1秒内，加速超过5700倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NILM自适应分割方法存在启发式参数调优、边界敏感性和指标饱和等问题，缺乏统一高效的自动化分割与评估框架。

Method: BayesSeg采用三层架构：(1)分割层：基于前段子序列尾值和均值的双稳态准则，结合顺序提取与互补集解析策略实现无监督分割；(2)评估层：将分割结果映射为二元状态序列，构建事件级F1(event_F1，通过容差匹配量化开关事件的精确率和召回率)与NMI(捕获全局结构一致性)的复合指标；(3)优化层：以复合分数为目标函数，采用TPE代理模型的贝叶斯优化进行全局参数空间探索。
